# Contract registry (RD-0359)

> Generated by `scripts/gen-contract-registry.mjs` — every governed `.fungi` flow contract in one place.
> Flow list is parser-authoritative (`parseProgram`); the `intent` string is extracted from source at the parser's flow location.
